2026-10-21T05:37:53.3485677Z   QWEN_COMMIT_MESSAGE: [cluster:firmus] [nodes:2] [profile] Serve the 2-math-warpgroup MegaMoE together with hot-4 expert replication

Why: two independent Prefill MegaMoE changes each beat the previous main
(8258.34) in separate serving runs:
- 2-math-warpgroup MegaMoE with the per-warp FC1 epilogue: 8356.18
  (39447708936), merged into main as bc4e5c7. It speeds up the GEMM part
  (kernel bench -3.6%).
- Hot-4 expert replication: 8360.73 (39479904180, run on the pre-2WG main).
  The top 4 experts per layer are copied to every Prefill rank (128 + 12
  = 140 physical experts, 35 per rank), and static dispatch prefers the
  same-GPU copy. This cuts NVLink traffic and evens expert load. On
  measured routing it was -5% to -8.5% MegaMoE (kernel benches 39462905249
  and 39477648172). TTFT mean was 1568 ms and prefill-0 input throughput
  14557 tokens/s.
The first acts on compute and the second on communication and expert
imbalance, so their gains should add.

What: this merges experiment/firmus-megamoe-replication-20261020 into main
bc4e5c7. Main is the first parent, so select-qwen-container.sh finds the
913f7bb build of image 39379223361.
- From main: image 39379223361 (rebuilt DeepGEMM host),
  sm90-prefill-2wg-host-epi.patch in deep_gemm_overlay_patches,
  DG_SM90_PREFILL_2WG=1 for Prefill workers.
- From the replication branch:
  - SGLang overlay megamoe-prefill-replicas.patch;
  - configs/firmus-prefill-replica4-expert-location.json (94 x 140);
  - prefill.ep_num_redundant_experts 12, passed by sglang.sbatch to Prefill
    only;
  - the replica4 logical-128 / physical-140 gate in
    check-firmus-megamoe.py (via firmus_megamoe_replicas.py);
  - source and server-info checks and tests.
  The branch's kernel-bench candidate directory is not carried over.
Nothing else changes: SGLang overlays 1-2, EAGLE3 3/4, Decode cap 128,
concurrency 192, KV settings. Decode keeps 0 redundant experts.

Risk: the 2-warpgroup header has not run with 35 experts per rank. The
host picks experts-per-wave = local experts at 4k tokens, so
kL2ActsSF128 and kPrefill2WG still apply. A separate 1-node kernel bench
from this commit checks the 2-warpgroup candidate with the physical-140
replica gate.

Expected: about +1% on top of each change alone, i.e. roughly 8400-8450.

  (39447708936), merged into main as bc4e5c7. It speeds up the GEMM part
  (kernel bench -3.6%).
- Hot-4 expert replication: 8360.73 (39479904180, run on the pre-2WG main).
  The top 4 experts per layer are copied to every Prefill rank (128 + 12
  = 140 physical experts, 35 per rank), and static dispatch prefers the
  same-GPU copy. This cuts NVLink traffic and evens expert load. On
  measured routing it was -5% to -8.5% MegaMoE (kernel benches 39462905249
  and 39477648172). TTFT mean was 1568 ms and prefill-0 input throughput
  14557 tokens/s.
The first acts on compute and the second on communication and expert
imbalance, so their gains should add.
